10.	paṭiyāṭciyit (Stratocracy) taṭukkāmi.
nalla mantiriyātaṟku evvakuppāṉ takuntava ṉeṉṟu viṉaviya oru cōḻaṉukku.
nūleṉilō kōlcāyum nuntamarēl veñcamarām
 kōleṉilō āṅkē kuṭicāyum - nālāvāṉ 
 mantiriyu māvāṉ vaḻikkut tuṇiyāvāṉ
 anta aracē aracu
eṉṟu ōr auviyār viṭiyiṟuttirukkiṉṟār. 
caṭṭat tuṟi (Legislature), niṟivēṟṟat tuṟi (Executive), tīrpputtuṟi (Judiciary) eṉṉum mūṉṟaṉuḷ talimi yāṉatu caṭṭattuṟi. niṟivēṟṟattuṟi āḷviṉip pakkam paṭittuṟip pakkam eṉa iru pakkaṅkaḷik koṇṭatu. āḷviṉikkut tuṇiyākavum karuviyākavumiruppatu paṭit tuṟi. ākavē, paṭittalivaṉ āḷviṉit talivaṉātal kūṭātu. orukāl āyiṉ, aṭuttaṭuttup pōr ceyvāṉ; allatu aḻippāṉ. ati iṉṟum kaṇṇārak kāṇ kiṉṟōm. itaṟku ekipatiyak kuṭiyaracut talivar pōṉṟa oru cilarē vilakku.
paṭittuṟi yāṭciyāl oru nāṭṭiṟ paṭukolikaḷ nērumpōtu, atit taṭukkum āṟṟaliṉṟi, oru nāṭṭiṉ akaviyaṟ ceytiyil (Internal affairs) taliyiṭakkūṭā teṉpatu, oruvaṉ taṉ maṉiviyiyō makkaḷiyō kollumpōtu, atu avaṉ contavurimi yeṉṟu colva tottatē.
   	koṭuṅkōlāṭciyi vilakkāmiyuṅ kuṟṟamē.
11.	vallaracar varuvāyiṟ perumpakutiyi aḻippu viṉikkē payaṉpaṭutti varutal.
ulakiṟ kālvācip pēr kuṭikkak kūḻiṟkum, kaṭṭak kantikkum,  kuṭiyirukkak kuṭicikkum vaḻiyaṟṟirukkum pōtu, ilakkavarik kolliyum nāṭaḻippāṉumākiya aṇuk kuṇṭukaḷiyum kaṇṭanturuvikaḷiyum (I.C.B.M.) kōṭik kaṇakkākac celaviṭṭuk kuṉṟupōṟ kuvittuk koṇṭē, ulakamiti yoppantaṅkaḷiyum ceytu varutal, amiti vēṇṭumeṉil amarukku aṇiya māyiru. (“If you want peace, be prepared for war.”) eṉṉum paḻiya neṟimuṟiyiyē kaṭip piṭippa tākiṉṟatu.
orukāl, makkaṭ perukkattik kuṟikka itu kuṟukku vaḻipōlum! ivvaḻiyāl ellārum māḷiṉ eṉṉa payaṉ? 
vallaracukaḷ, nilam kaṭal vāṉ ākiya mūviṭattilum aṇukkuṇṭukaḷi nōṭṭañceytum, ottiki pārttum, uṇavu kuṭinīr mūccuvaḷi ākiya iṉṟiyamiyāta mūvuṭ kōḷiyum nañcūṭṭutal. 
cuṟṟuccārput tīṭṭuppaṭuttam (Environment Pollution) paṟṟi ettaṉiyō mānāṭukaḷ naṭattikkoṇṭu, maṇṇil viḷiyum uṇavup poruḷkaḷiyum, nilattil vāḻum vilaṅkukaḷiyum, nīril vāḻum mīṉ iṟāl mutaliyavaṟṟi yum, vāṉil iyaṅkum kāṟṟiyum atiṟ paṟakkum paṟavika ḷiyum, nañcāka māṟṟivarutal, ettuṇit tuṇiccalāṉa māymālam!
māntaṉ mūviṭaṅkaḷiyum tīṭṭuppaṭuttik koṇṭu tiṅkaḷi yaṭiyiṉum cevvāyi yaṭiyiṉum, nākarikaṉu mākāṉ, paṇpaṭṭavaṉu mākāṉ.
13.	eṇṇey viliyuyarvit taṭukkum āṟṟaliṉmi
maṇṇeyum (Kerosine) kaṉṉeyum (Petrol) iyaṟkiyāka maṇṇil viḷivaṉa. ovvoru nilap pakutiyilum ovvoru poruḷ ciṟappāka viḷikiṉṟatu allatu kiṭikkiṉṟatu allatu ceyyappaṭukiṉṟatu. ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum ellā nāṭṭārum tammuṭ koṇṭuṅ koṭuttum parimāṟik koḷḷal vēṇṭum. uṇavup poruḷ maṭṭum viḷiyum nāṭukaḷ, maṇṇe yum kaṉṉeyum illāmalum kālantaḷḷa lām. āyiṉ, av vīreṇṇey maṭṭum viḷiyum nāṭukaḷ uṇavup poruḷum virivūrti kaḷum nākarikat taṭṭumuṭṭu kaḷum illāmal vāḻa muṭiyātu. ātalāl, eṇṇey viḷiyum nāṭukaḷellām oṉṟu cērntu koṇṭu, varampiṉṟi mēṉmēlum ataṉ viliyi uyarttik koṇṭu pōvatu nērmiyaṉṟu.
14.	ūrtimaṭakkali (hijacking) oḻikka oṟṟumiyiṉmi
kuṟṟañ ceytavaṉ caṭṭappaṭi taṇṭaṉiyaṭikiṉ ṟāṉ. ceṅkōlāṭciyil maṉṉaṉ makaṉum kuṟṟavāḷi yāyiṉ taṇṭaṉiyaṭital vēṇṭum. ettuṇi atikāra mum paṇakkoṭiyum atit taṭukkaviyalātu. āyiṉ ikkālattil ōr eḷiya kayavaṉ kaṭuṅkuṟṟañ ceytu ciṟit taṇṭaṉiyēyaṭiyiṉum, avaṉ naṇpaṉāṉa maṟṟoru kayavaṉ oru vāṉūrtiyiyō toṭarvaṇṭiyiyō maṭakki, kuṟṟa maṟṟavarum toṭar pillātavarumāṉa peṇṭirum ciṟuvaru muḷḷiṭṭa nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa paṉṉāṭṭu makkaḷi oru poṟit tumukki (machine gun) muṉ niṟuttikkoṇṭu, taṉ naṇpaṉi uṭaṉē viṭutali ceyyāviṭiṉ avaraṉi variyum orē noṭiyiṟ cuṭṭuk kolvatāka accuṟuttuvatu, koṭumiyiṟ koṭumiyākum. vallaracukaḷ uṭpaṭa 135 nāṭukaḷ kūṭiya oṉṟiya nāṭṭiṉaṅ kaḷ,  ūrtimaṭakkiya oru taṉippaṭṭa ciṟu kayavaṉiyum vella vaḻiyiṉṟic ceyalaṟṟu varuntuvataṟku, oṟṟumiyiṉmiyē karaṇiyam. ūrti maṭakkiyavaṉi maṭṭumaṉṟi avaṉukku  uṭantiyāyirunta nāṭukaḷiyum kaṭumiyāyt taṇṭittal vēṇṭum.
iṉi, māperuñ celvam virintu kavartaṟku ūrti maṭakkalum, piḷḷi kaḷavāṭalum (kidnapping), āṭkavarvum, iṟṟiyaracukaḷ  vilakkamuṭiyāta kolik koḷḷi kaḷākum.
kaṭitak kuṇṭukaḷ (letter bombs), cippak kuṇṭukaḷ (parcel bombs) mutaliyaṉa viṭuttal, kaṭṭaṭattiṟku uḷḷum puṟampum vaḻiyilum nērak kuṇṭukaḷip (time bombs) patittal, taṇṭavāḷaṅkaḷip peyarttal, etirkkaṭciyārit tākkutal, atikārikaḷiyum amiccarkaḷiyum añcal vāyilākavum tolipēci vāyilākavum moṭṭik kūṟṟāka accuṟuttal mutaliya palvaki accuṟuttatti aṭiyōṭu nīkkutal vēṇṭum.
15.	oru nāṭṭār iṉṉoru nāṭṭiṟ kalvikaṟkavō paṇiyāṟṟavō cuṟṟip pārkkavō iyalāmi.
16.	uṟuppu nāṭukaḷ kūṭṭuc ceyaliṉṟit taṉittaṉiyāka ovvoru nāṭṭuṭaṉ oppantañ ceytukoḷḷutal.
17.	pōri niṟuttāmi.
18.	paṭikkuṟippiṉmi.
19.	māntaṉ vāḻkkiyil maṉavamitiyiṉmi.
celvañ ciṟanta nāṭukaḷilum taṟkoli mikuntu varuvatāl, ētō oru kuṟi cilar maṉatti vāṭṭi varuvatākat terikiṉṟatu. atik kaṇṭupiṭittu atit tīrttal vēṇṭum.
20.	aintu moḻikaḷil oṉṟiya nāṭukaḷiṉ naṭapaṭikkikaḷ naṭantu varuvatu oṟṟumikkup poruntāmi.
ēṟkeṉavē pala moḻikaḷ naṭappu moḻikaḷā yiruppatāl, arapiyar arapi moḻiyiyum intiyār inti moḻiyiyum avaṟṟoṭu cērttu irukkuṅ kuḻappattip pāpēl pōṟ perukkak karutukiṉṟaṉar. ulakap potuk kaḻakattiṟku oru moḻiyē iruttal vēṇṭum. ataṟku ellā vakiyilum ēṟṟatu āṅkilam oṉṟē.
21. māntaṉ varalāṟu cariyāka eḻutappaṭāmi
oṉṟiya nāṭṭiṉaṅkaḷic cērnta kalviyaṟiviyaṟ paṇpāṭṭu amippakam (UNESCO), maṉpati varalāṟu (History of Mankind) 6 maṭalaṅkaḷākat tokuttu veḷi yiṭṭuḷḷatu. ataṉ mutaṉ maṭalattil māntaṉ piṟantakam tiṭṭavaṭṭamāyk kūṟappaṭavilli.  ovvoru mānta ṉūlārāycciyāḷarum, perumpālum naṭu nilimiyiṉṟit tāntām virumpiyavāṟē ovvōriṭattik kuṟittuḷḷaṉar.
pērā. ekkēl (Prof. Haeckel) maṭakācukkart tīvup pakkatti māntaṉ piṟantakamākak kuṟittār. iṉṟu teṉṉāp pirikkāvil māntaṉūlārāycci ceytuvarum ilīkki (Leakey), keṉyāvi māntaṉ tōṉṟiya iṭamākak karutukiṉṟār. iv vīriṭattiyum aṭut tirunta kumari nāṭē, māntaṉ piṟantakamum tamiḻaṉ tōṉṟiya iṭamu mākum.
22.	oppiyaṉ moḻinūl (Comparative Philology) uṇmiyāṉa vaḷarcci peṟāmi.
mēliyar, iyaṉmoḻiyākiya tamiḻi ārāyātu, tirimoḻiyiṉ tirimoḻiyākiya vētamoḻiyiyum ataṉ ilakkiya vaḷarcciyāṉa camaṟkirutattiyum inti rōppiya moḻikaṭku aṭippaṭiyāka vittārāyntataṉāl, mūla moḻiyik kaṇṭupiṭikka muṭiyātu, varalāṟṟi vilakkiya vaṇṇaṉi moḻi nūlit tavaṟāka vaḷarttu varukiṉṟaṉar. itaṟku avaratu āriya veṟiyē karaṇiyam.
7. ulaka oṟṟumikku vaḻikaḷ
1. ulakap potu āṭci (International Govt.) amittal
makkaḷellārum ōriṉam eṉṟum ulaka muḻuvatum oru māperuṅ kuṭumpameṉṟum oppukkoṇṭu, ovvoru varum tammi ulakak kuṭimakaṉār allatu makaḷār (world citizen) eṉṟu otta uṭaṉpiṟappākak karuti yoḻukutal vēṇṭum.
niyūyārkkiyē aracirukkiyākak koṇṭu, oṉṟiya nāṭṭiṉaṅkaḷ amippakatti ulakāḷumaṉṟamāka (International Parliament) māṟṟi, ellā nāṭukaḷiyum ataṉ uṟuppākkutal vēṇṭum. intiyā pōṉṟa peru nāṭukaṭku aintu uṟuppāṇmi (Membership) aḷikkalām. cīṉam pōṉṟa māperu nāṭukaṭkup pattuvariyum aḷikka lām.
ovvoru nāṭṭilum, avvan nāṭṭumakkaḷē ulakāḷu maṉṟa vuṟuppiṉarāka oruvariyō aivariyō tērnteṭuttal vēṇṭum. tērnteṭukkappaṭṭa uṟuppi ṉarē tamakkuḷ oruvarit talivarākat tērnteṭuttal vēṇṭum. at talivarē talimi  mantiriyārāyiruntu, piṟa mantirimārit  terintu koḷvār . ulakāḷu maṉṟattiṟku ōr araṅkē (Chamber) pōtum; kīḻavi mēlaviyeṉṟu īraraṅku tēviyilli.
talimi mantiriyār, ulakāḷumaṉṟa vuṟuppirik koṇṭu maṉṟat talivarit tērnteṭuttuk koḷḷal vēṇṭum.
ovvoru kaṇṭattiṟkum ulakāṭcit tuṇit talivar oruvar iruttal vēṇṭum. amerikkāvi iru kaṇṭamākak karutalām. ovvoru tuṇit talivariyum avvak kaṇṭa nāṭukaḷē tērnteṭuttal vēṇṭum. tuṇittalivar aṟuvarum tammuḷ oruvarit tali varākat tērnteṭuttal vēṇṭum.
ulakāḷu maṉṟam oṉṟiya nāṭṭiṉaṅkaḷiṉ potup pēravi pōlum, ulakāṭci mantiricapi oṉṟiya nāṭṭiṉaṅ kaḷiṉ kāppuṟuti maṉṟam pōlum ceyaṟpaṭa vēṇṭum.
ceyalāḷakamum oṉṟu amital vēṇṭum. atu kuṭimit (Civil) tuṟi, paṭit (Military) tuṟi eṉṟu iru piriviṉatāyiruttal vēṇṭum.
ulakat tīrppu maṉṟam iṉṟu ēkil (Hague) uḷḷatē toṭaralām.
ulakanāṭukaḷiṉ vaḻakkamāṉa tērtalum āṭci muṟiyum iṉṟupōl eṉṟum toṭarum. āyiṉ, cila atikāraṅ kaḷum urimikaḷum kuṟiyum. oru makkaḷāṭcik kūṭṭaraciṉ naṭuvaṇaracum nāṭṭaracum pōṉṟu, ulakap potu āṭciyum ulaka nāṭāṭciyum irukkum.
ulakamuḻutum nāṭāṭcit tērtalkaḷ orē camiyattil nikaḻum. ataṉ piṉ, ulakāṭcit tērtalum orē camiyattil nikaḻum.
nāṭāṭcikaḷil kāvaltuṟiyaṉṟip paṭittuṟi yirātu. ulakāṭci yamippilēyē māperu valimiyuḷḷa muttuṟip paṭiyirukkum. atu neṟitavaṟum nāṭṭit taṇṭikkap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum.
pōr aṭiyōṭu oḻikkappaṭum. ātalāl. vallaracukaḷ tēkki vittuḷḷa pēraḻippup paṭik kalaṅkaḷum kuṇṭukaḷum aṭiyōṭu aḻikkappaṭum.
pōr oḻikkappaṭṭu viṭuvatāl, kallūrikaḷil māṇavar paṭittuṟip payiṟciyum irātu. paṭittuṟip payiṟci yirukkumvari, pōr maṉappāṉmiyum paki yuṇarcciyum, ciṉaveḻucciyum iruntukoṇṭē yirukkum. pōrk kuṇaṅkaḷ ettuṇic ciṟantaṉavāyiṉum pēyikat tiṉ pāṟpaṭṭaṉavē. oruvaṉ taṉ maṟatti, naṟkoḷkiyik kaṭippiṭippatilum oḻukkattip pēṇuvatilum tuṉpattip poṟuttalilum piṟarkku naṉmi ceytalilumē kāṭṭal vēṇṭum.
1.	ulakāṭciyil āṅkilam oṉṟē āṭcimoḻiyāyirukkum.
2.	ulaka muḻutum orē kācuttiṭṭamum ōriyal aḷavi vakikaḷum ēṟpaṭal.
3.	ellāp poruḷkaṭkum nērmiyāṉa vili kuṟikkap paṭalum vilivāci yēṟṟamiṉmiyum.
4.	ēṟṟumatiyum iṟakkumatiyum iḻappiṉṟiyum iṭi yaṟātum naṭipeṟa ēṟpāṭu ceyyappaṭutal.
 5.	nāṭiḻantavarkku nāṭum,  vīṭiḻantavarkku vīṭum tiruppap paṭal.
 6.	iṉaveṟi kulaveṟi mataveṟi moḻiveṟi nāṭṭuveṟi mutaliya ellā veṟikaḷum aṭiyōṭu oḻikkappaṭutal.
	ūrveṟiyum vaṭṭāraveṟiyum pōṉṟatē nāṭṭu veṟiyum. yātum ūrē yāvaruṅ kēḷir eṉṟu ulaka muḻuvatiyum orē nāṭākak koḷḷutal vēṇṭum.
 7.	oru nāṭṭār maṟṟoru nāṭṭāroṭu maṇavuṟavu koḷvataṟkāṉa vaḻivakikaḷi vakuttal.
 8.	oruvar ennāṭṭiyuñ cuṟṟippārkka ētun taṭiyirāmi.
 9.	celva nāṭukaḷ eḷiya nāṭukaṭku ellā vakiyilum utavi ceytal.
10.	ellārum vāḻavēṇṭum eṉṉum potunalak koḷ kiyi eṅkum parapputal.
8. ulakak kūṭṭāṭciyiṉ naṉmikaḷ
1.	ulaka amitiyum oṟṟumiyum.
2.	nōyaṟṟa makkaḷ neṭunal vāḻvu.
3.	makkaḷ camaṉmi.
4.	kaviṉ kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷum māṭamāḷiki kūṭakōpuraṅ kaḷum vāṉaḷāvikaḷum arunūlkaḷum tol poruḷ kaḷum kākkappaṭal.
5.	nāṭukaḷiṉ celavukkuṟivu.
6.	viḷivupperukkam.
7.	vaṟumi nīkkam.
8.	aṟivu vaḷarcci.
9.	paṇpāṭṭu vaḷarcci.
10.	iṟumpūtup putuppuṉivākkam.
11.	kaṭalkōḷ, veḷḷam mutaliya ārkalic cētak kuṟippu.
12.	makkaṭṭoki maṭṭuppāṭu.
13.	pālinilap paṇpaṭuttam.
14.	kāriṉa māntar tōṟṟattiruttam.
15.	makkaḷiṉa vuyarvu.
16.	kaṭalnīrik kuṭinīrākkal, katiravaṉoḷiyic camiyaṟ kup payaṉpaṭuttal mutaliya māṟṟaṅkaḷ.
ittakiya viṇṇaka iṉpa vāḻvi maṇṇakattil niṟuvutaṟku, ulaka mutunāṭākiya intiyāvē ulaka mutu moḻiyākiya centamiḻit tuṇikkoṇṭu, virintu aṭikōla vēṇṭum.
6
muṭivuri
1. intiyāviṉ mupperuṅ kēṭukaḷi nīkkal
1. piṟavikkulap piriviṉi yoḻippu
intiyar  nalvāḻviṟkum muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkum oṟṟu mikkum valutta muṭṭukkaṭṭiyiṭuvatum kāntiyaṭika ḷālum cavakarlāl nēruvālum nīkkamuṭiyātatum, intiya viṭutali yip poruḷaṟṟa tākac ceyvatum, maruntillā māperuṅ kumukāyak koṭunōyākat toṭarvatum, cilar karutuvatu pōl, matuvaruntalaṉṟu; piṟavik kulappirivi ṉiyē. kaṭukaḷavum poruttamiṉṟip piṟappāṟ ciṟappu vakuttuk kumukāyatti nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa kulaṅkaḷākap pirippatum, avaṟṟiyellām paṭimuṟiyēṟṟat tāḻvu paṭuttip pirāmaṇak kulatti ellāvaṟṟiṟkum mēlāka vippatum, piṟa kulaṅkaḷiyellām muṉṉēṟṟa miṉṟip piṟappumutal iṟappuvari atataṟkuriya orē niliyil niṟuttuvatum, āṉa ivvanākarika iḻitakavuk kumukāya vakupputtiṭṭam intiyāviṟkē ciṟappākum. intiyā cappāṉip pōṉṟu mēṉāṭukaḷip piṉpaṟṟiyēṉum itu vari muṉṉēṟāmikkum, iṉṟum aṅṅaṉam muṉṉēṟum nilimiyiliṉ mikkum, ituvē karaṇiyamāyiruppatāl, evvakic cākkup pōkkuṅ kūṟik kaṭatti viyātu uṭaṉaṭiyāy iti aṟavē oḻittal vēṇṭum. iṉṟēl, kūṭṭuṭimiyāṭciyiltāṉ iḵti yalum.
ayalār āṭciyeṉṉum āṅkilarāṭciyiltāṉ, tāḻttap paṭṭavariṉ nilimi tiruntat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. āṅkilar nīṅki 25 āṇṭāṉa piṉpum, vaṟumiyum nīṅka villi; tīṇṭāmiyum oḻiyavilli. kūli kūṭṭikkēṭkum ēḻi yuḻippāḷiyar iṉṟum cuṭṭum erittum kollappaṭu kiṉṟaṉar. āṅkilar kāl nūṟṟāṇṭu muntic ceṉṟu viṭṭaṉar eṉpatē naṭuniliyaṟiñar karuttu.
2. varalāṟṟu maṟippu nīkkam
āriyar varukikku muṟpaṭṭa mutaliru kaḻakat taṉittamiḻ nūlkaḷ aṉittum aḻikkappaṭṭuviṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟiṟkup piṟpaṭṭaṉavum pala iṟantupaṭṭaṉa. iṉṟiruppavi yellām maṟuviḷiccal (after-growth) pōṉṟaviyē.
kumarināṭṭut tamiḻē tiraviṭa moḻikaṭkellām tāyum āriya moḻikaṭkellām mūlamum ākum. iṟṟit tamiḻum paranta paltuṟip paṇṭi yilakkiyattiyum pallāyirak kaṇakkāṉa coṟkaḷiyum iḻantapiṉpum, iyaṉmiyilum tūymiyilum kumarittamiḻip peritum ottatē. teṉ moḻikkē ciṟappāṉa ilakkaṇamum ilakkiyamum iṉṟum tamiḻilēyē uḷḷaṉa. vēṟet tiraviṭa moḻiyilum illi. ātalāl, araciyalamippu muṟiyiṟ kācumīrattiṟkut taṉiccaluki kāṭṭiyatu pōṉṟē, moḻit tuṟiyil tamiḻnāṭṭiṟkun taṉiccaluki kāṭṭutal vēṇṭum.
kōvil vaḻipāṭu tamiḻil naṭipeṟutal, āṭpeyar ūrppeyar kaṭṭaṭap peyar poruṭpeyar mutaliyaṉa taṉittamiḻc coṟkaḷāyiruttal, tamiḻnāṭṭup palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷil tamiḻē mutaṉmoḻiyākavum talimi moḻi yākavum pōṟṟappaṭal, āṅkilap paṭṭam peṟṟavarum tamiḻp paṟṟuḷḷavarumāṉa tamiḻppulavarē tamiḻnāṭṭup palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷil tuṇi vēyntarāka amarttappeṟal, tamiḻ nāṭṭu aracu tiṇikkaḷat (Departmental) talivarum varalāṟṟāciriyarum tolporuḷāyvut talivarum tamiḻp paṟṟāḷarākavēyiruttal, ceṉṉiyilēṉum tirunelvēli yilēṉum tamiḻp palkalikkaḻakam tōṟṟuvittal, camaṟ kirutattiṟkum mūlamāṉa tamiḻiṉ vaḷarccikku naṭuvaṇaracāl oru kōṭi yurupāvēṉum otukkappaṭal, ulakap palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷellāvaṟṟilum tamiḻp pērāciriyap pataviyēṟ paṭal, oṉṟiya nāṭṭiṉaṅkaḷil (UN) āṇṭiṟ korunāḷ ulaka mutaṉmoḻi nāḷ eṉṉum peyarāl tamiḻttirunāḷ koṇ ṭāṭappaṭal, tamiḻnāṭṭu etirkāla āḷnarellām iṟṟi yāḷnar perumāṇpumiku kē.kē.cā pōlat tamiḻ kaṟṟavarākavum tamiḻp paṟṟuṭiyā rākavumiruttal, etirkāla intiyak kuṭiyaracu talivarkkum talimi mantiriyārkkum paṟṟuṭaṉ kūṭiya ōraḷavu tamiḻkkalvi takutiyāyiruttal, ayal nāṭu kaḷiluḷḷa tamiḻt toṭarpi āyntu nūleḻutiyavarkkut takunta paricaḷittal mutaliyaṉa tamiḻuyarttattiṟkum parappaṟkum iṉṟiyamiyātaṉa vākum.
2. tamiḻāṅkila nerukkam
e-ṭu: 
iyaṟki moḻi (Natural Language or Inarticulate Speech)
ā - ah! im - imil - imiḻ - hum.
uraṟu - OE. rarian, E. roar, M.Du. reer.
ella, ellā -  hello, hallo, hollo.
X - O!, oh!
kari - OE. craw, E. crow; ME. cri, E. cry, L. quirito.
kū, kūvu - coo.
cil - chill.
taṭa, taṭār - thud.
piḷiṟu - ME. bler, E. blare, M.Du. blar.
miyā - mew.
vikkal - hicket, hiccup.
vaḷarccimoḻi (Developed Language or Articulate Speech)
āṉ - yon, āṇṭu - yond, yonder (comp. deg.).
itō - itōḷ, itōḷi - OE. hider, E. hither, Goth. hidre.
kol - ME. culle, kille, E. kill.
camaṭṭu - OE. smit, M.Du. smit, Goth. smeit, E. smite.
JUî - OE., OS. thurh, Goth. thirh, E. through.
nāku(iḷaṅkutiri) - E. nag. M.Du. negghe.
bghW - OE., OS. ber, Goth. bir, E. bear.
maṉ - OS. man, Goth. manna, E. man.
vicukku - E. whisk, ME. wisk, Sw. viska.
kuṟippu:	ivi taṉṉērccic coṟkaḷalla. ivaṟṟip pōṉṟa aṭippaṭic coṟkaḷ iṉṉum pala vuḷa. mēliyāriyariṉ muṉṉōrāṉa tiraviṭar mutaṉ mutal airōppāviṉ vaṭamēṟ pākattiyaṭintata ṉāl, aṅkut tamiḻccoṟkaḷ peritun tiriyāmaṟ pōṟṟappaṭṭu vantirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉa aṟika.
ivaṟṟiyāriya moḻikaḷuḷ, mēlik kōṭiyiluḷ ḷatu āṅkilam; kīḻikkōṭiyil uḷḷatu ilakkiya moḻiyāṉa camaṟkirutam. camaṟkirutattiṟkut tamiḻ mūlam eṉṉum uṇmi muṉṉarē kāṭṭappaṭṭuviṭṭatu.
tamiḻ āriyattiṟku mūlameṉpatu aiyuṟappaṭiṉ, ulakap paṭṭimaṉṟam oṉṟil atu aiyantiripaṟa nāṭṭappaṭum. tamiḻ vaṭamoḻip piṇippiṉiṉṟu mīṭkappaṭṭāloḻiyat tamiḻ vāḻvumilli; tamiḻaṉ vāḻvumilli.
tamiḻ teṉṉāṭṭup paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ eṉpatu, ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻaka varalāṟṟuttuṟit talivarā yirunta (P.T.) cīnivāciyaṅkārālum (V.R.) irāmaccantira tīṭcitarālum maṟukka muṭiyāta nūlvāyilāka niṟuvappaṭṭa piṉpum, av vuṇmiyic cila tamiḻp pakivar maṟittu varu kiṉṟaṉar. itaṟkuc ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakamum uṭanti yākavuḷḷatu.
āṅkilarāṭci ēṟpaṭumvari intiyā orukālum ōrāṭci nāṭāka iruntatilli. 1857-l kiḷarnta vaṭanāṭṭup porunar kalakam (Sepoy Mutiny), tilli  mukalāya aracarum kuvāliyar araciyum marāttiyap pīcuvāvum (Peshwa) tūṇṭi viṭṭa araciyaṟ pōrēyaṉṟit tēciyap pōrāṭṭamaṉṟu.
āṅkilak kiḻakkintiyak kuḻumpār (East India Company) vaṇikañ ceyya vantavarēyaṉṟi, nāṭukavarap paṭiyeṭuttu vantavarallar. akkālattuc cūḻnilikaḷ avarip paṭippaṭi yāka intiya aracarākkiviṭṭaṉa. mutaṟkaṇ, avarit  tākkiṉa ciṟṟaracukaḷ tōlviyuṟṟuk koṭutta taṇṭattiṉālum, avaratu (āṅkilaratu) utavipeṟṟup pōril veṉṟa aracukaḷ koṭutta naṉkoṭiyiṉālum, cila nilappakutikaḷip peṟṟaṉar. piṉṉar, kāpputavi yoppantam (Subsidiary Alliance), piṟaṅkaṭiyiṉṟi        araciḻappu (Doctrine of Lapse), ūḻalāṭci nāṭṭik kavartal (Annexation of Mis-ruled States), pōr veṟṟi mutaliya  vaḻikaḷāl intiyāvil 1/3 paṅki aṭintu viṭṭaṉar. ati akkālattiṟ pala intiyat talivar kaṇṭittaṉar. āṭu koḻuttāl āyaṉukku ūtiyam eṉpati avar aṟiya villi. intiyāvil 1/3 paṅkik kippaṟṟi, 545 uḷnāṭṭaracukaḷiyum aṭakki avaṟṟip paṭi kuṟikkac ceytu, oru māperum paṭi yamittup pērāyat talivariṭam āṅkilar oppaṭit tirāviṭiṉ, nēru nicāmi (Nizam) veṉṟirukkavē muṭiyātu; uḷnāṭṭara cellām oṉṟu cērntiruppiṉ, āṅkilar nēraṭiyāka āṇṭa nilappakutikaḷiyum peṟṟirukka muṭiyātu. ākavē, intiyāvi mutaṉmutalāka oṉṟupaṭuttik kuṭiyaracu nāṭākkit tanta perumi, āṅkilariyē muṟṟum cārum.
iṉi, ari nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkumēṟ pōrāṭi muḻu viṭutali peṟṟa intiyat tēciyap pērāyattit tōṟṟu vittavarē, ōr āṅkilar eṉpati iṉṉum māperumpālār aṟiyavilli; aṟintavarum, orucilar tavirap piṟar uṇara villi.
1931-l ilaṇṭaṉil naṭipeṟṟa 2ām vaṭṭamēci mānāṭṭil, pērātaṉ (makātmā) kāntiyaṭikaḷ piṉvarumāṟu kūṟiyirukkiṉṟār:
nāṉ intiyat tēciyap pērāyattiṉ cārpākac ceya lāṟṟum ōr ēḻit tāḻmiyāṉa mukavaṉ. pērāyam ettakiya teṉṟum, etaṟkāka ēṟpaṭṭateṉṟum, nām niṉivuṟuttik koḷvatu nalamākum. ataṉpiṉṉē nīṅkaḷ eṉpāṟ kaṇṇōṭṭaṅ kāṭṭuvīrkaḷ. ēṉeṉiṉ, eṉmēṟ cumarn tuḷḷa cumati uṇmiyil mikap periyatu. pērāyam intiyāvil emakkuḷḷa mikap  paḻamiyāṉa araciyalamip pakam eṉ patil oru tavaṟumilliyeṉṟu karutukiṉṟēṉ. atu tōṉṟi ēṟattāḻa 50 āṇṭākiṉṟatu. ataṉ kālamellām iṭiyaṟātu āṇṭutōṟum mānāṭu kūṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atu ati oru tēciya amippakam  eṉṟu kāṭṭukiṉṟatu. atu entat taṉippaṭṭa iṉap piriviyāvatu, entat taṉippaṭṭa vakuppiyāvatu, entat taṉippaṭṭa viruppak kūṭṭattiyāvatu paṭinikarkkavilli. atu ellā intiyar naṉmikkākavum ellā vakuppiṟ kākavum pāṭupaṭak kōrukiṉṟatu. atit tōṟṟuvikkuṅ karuttu mutaṉmutal ōr āṅkila mūḷiyil uruvāyiṟ ṟeṉṟu colvatu, eṉakku māperu makiḻcciyit tarukiṉṟatu. ālaṉ ākattēviyaṉ kiyūm (Allan Octavian Hume) pērāyattiṉ tantiyeṉa yām aṟikiṉṟēm. -pērāya varalāṟu (The History of the Congress), pa. 30.
varalāṟeṉpatu muḻuttūymiyāṉa maṟinūl. matat toṭarpāṉa maṟinūlkaḷ ciṟitum peritum māṟṟiyeḻutap paṭalām. āyiṉ, varalāṟṟi ellāmvalla iṟivaṉum ōreḻuttum māṟṟa iyalātu; māṟṟiṉ poyyaṉāki iṟimiyiḻantuviṭuvāṉ. ātalāl, teyva accamuḷḷa naṭuniliyāḷarē varalāṟṟi varital vēṇṭum.
3. intiya āṭcimoḻittiṭṭam
kuṭiyaracu talivar, talimi mantiriyār, talimit tīrppāḷar uṭpaṭa ikkālak kaṟṟōrellām, āṅkilak kalviyāl aṟivaṭintavarē. avvaṟiviyakaṟṟum vaḻi yiruntu akaṟṟiṉ, avar kalvittoḻil oṉṟiṟkum utavār. av vaḻiyiṉmiyāl, peṟṟōri maṟuppavarum, cey naṉṟi kolpavarum tērtalil veṟṟipeṟṟapiṉ kaṭcimāṟupavarum pōl, āṅkilak kalviyi nīkkavum, ataṉāl etirkāla intiyari, ciṟappākat teṉṉintiyarik keṭukkavum tuṇikiṉṟaṉar.
intiya āṭcimoḻit tiṭṭam kīḻvarumāṟu iraṇṭil  oṉṟākattāṉ iruttal kūṭum:
1)āṅkilam oṉṟē niliyāṉa intiyap potumoḻi; allatu,
2)āṅkilam, tamiḻ, inti ākiya mūṉṟum intiyap potu moḻikaḷ.
potumoḻi mūṉṟāyiṉ, naṭuvaṇaracu mūṉṟilum naṭipeṟal vēṇṭum. uṟuppunāṭukaṭku am mūṉṟuḷ ētēṉum oṉṟit terintukoḷḷa muḻuvurimi vēṇṭum. iv viraṇṭil oṉṟu naṭipeṟākkāl, intiyaruḷ oṟṟumi yirātu. ataṉāl intiyā eṉṟum oru nāṭāyirātu. intiveṟiyar tamiḻnāṭṭil intiyit tiṇippiṉ, atu ayalāṭciyum (Xenocracy), koṭuṅ kōlāṭciyum (Despotism), taṉmūppāṭciyum (Dictatorship) ākavē karutappaṭum. tamiḻnāṭu āṅkilattiyē ēṟkum.
ulakap potumoḻi yamippu muyaṟci
moḻi makkaḷiṉantoṟum vēṟupaṭṭiruppataṉālum, palvēṟu nāṭṭiṉa makkaḷ oṉṟukūṭumpōtu ellārkkum viḷaṅkumāṟu pēcattakka potumoḻiyoṉṟiṉmi yālum, ceṉṟa (19ām) nūṟṟāṇṭil airōppiya moḻiyaṟiñar palar pala ceyaṟkip potumoḻikaḷi oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāka uṇṭupaṇṇip pārttaṉar. avi, vālappukku (Volapuk), ecupperēṉṟō (Esperanto), muṇṭaliṅkuve (Mundalingue), iṭiyam niyūṭṭiral (IdiomNeutral),  iṭō (Ido), ecuppe rēṉṟiṭō (Esperantido), iṉṟerliṅkuvā (Interlingua), nōviyal (Novial) mutaliyaṉa.
avaṟṟuḷ ovvoṉṟilum palvēṟu kuṟikaḷ iruntataṉālum, avaṟṟi amikkum muyaṟciyil īṭupaṭṭa varkaḷ perumpālum āṅkila amerikkarallātu airōppi yarākavēyiruntamiyālum, āṅkilam āṅkilarāṭciyāl ulakamuḻutum paraviviṭṭamiyālum, atu ilattīṉ ceru māṉiyam mutaliya airōppiya moḻikaḷiṉum eḷimiyāka amintiruttalālum, atuvē ulakap potumoḻiyātal cālumeṉa oruvāṟu kuṟippāka oppukkoḷḷappaṭṭu. oru ceyaṟkip putup potumoḻippuṉivu muyaṟci muṟṟuṅ kiviṭappaṭṭuviṭṭatu.
ākavē etirkālavulakil, putumoḻi vakiyil āṅki lamum mutumoḻi vakiyil tamiḻumē ulakap potumoḻi kaḷāka vaḷarntōṅkumeṉa aṟika. ulakap potumoḻiyē intiyap potumoḻiyākavum āṭci moḻiyākavum iruttal vēṇṭum.
2. vaḷḷuvar kūṭṭuṭimi
celvañ cērum vaḻikaḷ
nallavi: perumuyaṟci allatu pēruḻippu, vaṇikam, putiyal, muṉṉōr tēṭṭu (mutucom), piṟar cāvu, tirumaṇavarici, perumpatavi, ampakam (Permit), putuppuṉivu 
(Invention) allatu puttamippu, paricu, naṉkoṭi, matinuṭpam, kaṭṭaḻaku, matavalimi (Samsonhood).
tīyavi: kaḷavu, koḷḷi, kiyūṭṭu, cūtāṭṭu, vañcaṉi, koli, kaḷḷakkācaṭippu, kaḷḷakkaṭattam, kaḷḷa vāṇikam, kāṭṭik koṭuppu, parattimi.
iv viruvaki vaḻikaḷuḷḷum taliciṟantatu peru muyaṟciyoṉṟē. piṟavellām perumpālum taṉṉērcci (accident), vāyppu (chance), koḷḷi, vaṉ ceyal eṉṉum nālvakip paṭṭaṉavē.
uṟuppilikaḷum nōyāḷiyarum kaḷikaṇillāc ciṟuvarum poruḷīṭṭaliṉiṉṟu iyaṟkiyāl taṭukkappaṭṭa varāvar. kalvit tiṟamiyillār uṭampuḻippālēyē piḻit taṟkuriyār. tāḻttappaṭṭōr piṟa vakuppār kūṭṭuṟaviṉāṟ poruḷīṭṭum vaḻikaḷiṉiṉṟu tīṇṭāmi yāṟ perumpālum vilakkappaṭṭavarāvar.
perumuyaṟci ceytaṟku uṭalvalimi, nōyiṉmi, mativaṉmi, maṉavūkkam, iṭamporuḷēval, vāḻnāḷ nīṭippu, vāṉili, tiruvaruḷ mutaliya palavum ottuvaral vēṇṭum. ivi yellām ottuvaruvatē oru vāyppu vakiyākum.
ellārum perumuyaṟci ceytalum iyalātu. ellāruñ celvarāyiṉ ēvalceyya oruvarumirār. ellārum mutalāḷi kaḷāyiṉ toḻilāḷi oruvarumirāṉ. toḻilāḷi yutaviyiṉṟi mutalāḷi poruḷīṭṭa muṭiyātu. ātalāl, perum poruḷīṭṭum vāyppuḷḷava rellām, aḵtillāta ēḻimakkaḷuṭaṉ pakirntuṇṇutalum, irap pōrkku iṭṭuṇṇutalum   vēṇṭum.
iruppatu poy; iṟappatu mey. āṟiluñ cāvu; nūṟiluñ cāvu. tēvikku miñciṉa paṇamum iṟantapiṉ eñcum paṇamum vīṇ. ellārkkum vēliyum tēviyum kiṭip piṉ, kavalikkiṭamilli; piḷḷikaṭkut tēṭi vikkavum vēṇṭiyatilli. makkaṭṭokiyi maṭṭum maṭṭuppaṭuttal vēṇṭum. ellārkkum mūppuc campaḷam kiṭikkumpōtu peṟṟa makkaḷutaviyum vēṇṭiyatilli. māḷumvari māṉattoṭu vāḻalām.
makkaṭṭoki mikka kālattiṟku ēṟṟatu kūṭṭuṭimi yaracē. taṉiyuṭimi nāṭukaḷum iṉṟu paṭippaṭiyākak kūṭṭuṭimik koḷkikaḷi mēṟkoṇṭum kiyāṇ ṭum varukiṉṟaṉa. neṭuṅkālañ celliṉum, iṟutiyil amerikka oṉṟiya nāṭukaḷum (USA) kūṭṭuṭimiyi ēṟkattāṉ nērum.
kūṭṭuṭimit toṭakkak kālattil, atik ki yāḷum nāṭukaḷiṟ cila āṭcit tavaṟukaḷum kuṟipāṭukaḷum iruppatu iyalpē. avaṟṟit tiruttikkoḷḷa vēṇṭumē yaṉṟi, avaṟṟiyē aḷaviyākak koṇṭu avvāṭci muṟiyip  puṟakkaṇittal aṟivuṭimi yākātu. vaḷḷuvar kūṭṭuṭimi ellā nāṭukaṭkum iṉi ellāk kālattiṟkum ēṟṟatākum.
tiṟamikkut takka muyaṟci; tēvikkut takka nukarcci. ituvē kūṭṭuṭimi. itiṟ kuṟṟamoṉṟu milli.
3. ulakak kūṭṭaracu
oṉṟiya nāṭṭiṉaṅkaḷ (UN) uṇmiyil oṉṟāta nāṭṭiṉaṅkaḷāyiruppatāl, ulakak kūṭṭaracu iṉṟiyami yātatākum.
pōriyum ūrtimaṭakkaliyum niṟuttalum, ulaka makkaṭṭokiyik kuṟittalum, koṭuṅkōlāṭci yiyum iṉa mata moḻi veṟikaḷiyum vilakkalum, vaṟumi yiyum nōyiyum nīkkalum, kavaliyiyum tuṉpattiyum tīrttalum ulakak kūṭṭaracāltāṉ iyalum.
iṉṟu ulakameṅkum paraviyirukkum oru peruṅ koṭun tuṉpam makkaṭṭoki mikiyē. atik kuṟikkum vari, entak kaṭciyālum enta nāṭṭu utaviyālum enta aiyāṇṭut tiṭṭattālum entap potunaṉmiyum viḷiyātu. āḷuṅkaṭci, tērtal veṟṟiyi nōkkātu, koṭiya nōykkuk kaṭiya maruntu pōṉṟu, irumakavuk kuṭumpat tiṭṭatti uṭaṉaṭiyāy niṟivēṟṟal vēṇṭum.
4. tamiḻiṉ taṉiyiyalpu
iyaṟcoṟ ṟāmē
 centamiḻ nilattu vaḻakkoṭu civaṇit
 tamporuḷ vaḻāmi icikkucollē 	(tol. ecca. 2)   
eṉṟu, iyalpāṉa tamiḻccoṟkaḷellām centamiḻc collēyeṉa,  tolkāppiyaṅ kuṟikkiṉṟatu. 
āriyar teṉṉāṭu varumuṉ, tamiḻ muṟṟun taṉit tamiḻākavēyiruntatu. ataṉpiṉ, tamiḻccoṟkaḷi ovvoṉ ṟāka vaḻakku vīḻttavum iṟantupaṭac ceyyavumē, āriyac coṟkaḷ ovvoṉṟāka vēṇṭātu tamiḻiṟ pukuttappaṭṭaṉa. nakam (OE. nxgel, OS,OHG. nagal,ON. nagl, Skt. nakha, E. nil) eṉṉum āriyaccol pukuntu ukir eṉṉuñ colli vaḻakku vīḻttiyuḷḷamiyiyum, jaṉṉal eṉṉum pōrttuk kīciyac col pukuntatataṉāl palakaṇi, kālatar,  cāḷaram eṉṉum muttamiḻccoṟkaḷ vaḻakkiṟantuḷḷamiyiyum, nōkkuka.
piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷiyum piṟamoḻiyeḻuttu kaḷiyum tamiḻiṟ pukuttiṉ, tamiḻ nāḷaṭivil maliyāḷam pōl vēṟoru tiraviṭamoḻiyāka māṟiviṭum. ataṉpiṉ atit tamiḻeṉa vaḻaṅkuvatu avali niṉittukkoṇṭu urali iṭippatu pōṉṟatē.
vētamoḻiyilum camaṟkirutattil maṭṭumaṉṟi, mēli yāriya moḻikaḷilum nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa aṭippaṭic coṟkaḷum tiricoṟkaḷum iṉṟun tamiḻāyuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuṭ pala aṟiviyaṟ kuṟiyīṭukaḷumāyiruppatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.
e-ṭu:   1. E. atom(ic) bomb
Gk. a, not; temno, temo, to cut; atomos, indivisible. E. atom, particle of matter that cannot be further divided.
al-a (etirmaṟi muṉṉoṭṭu).  o.nō:  nal - na. kul - kuḷ - ku. tumital  = veṭṭuṇ ṇutal. tumittal = veṭṭutal. a + tumam = atumam - atum - atom.
E.  bomb, a shell for cannon. F.  bombe f. It. bomba, f. L.  bombus, a humming noise f. Gk. bombos, a humming or buzzing noise.
pommeṉal = ōr olikkuṟippu.
iṅṅaṉam atumappom eṉpatu, atom(ic) bomb eṉpati oliyum poruḷum mūlamum ottiruttal kāṇka.
2. E. television- tolikkāṭci
Gk. tele, far, distant, toli  -  tele.
L. video, to see, video - viso, to see, look at. visio, view, visum, vision, E. vide-vise-vision.
viḻittal = kaṇtiṟattal, pārttal, kāṇutal, aṟital.
viḻi = kaṇ, kāṭci, aṟivu(ñāṉam).
viḻi - L. vide. ḻa - ṭa, tiripu. o.nō: nāḻi - nāṭi, kōḻi - te. kōṭi.
ṭa - ca, pōlittiripu. e - ṭu: oṭi - oci, piṭi - pici.
3. E. pedigree - koṭivaḻi, koṭivaḻimarapu, koṭivaḻip paṭṭi.
AF., OF pie de grue, crane’s foot.
pati - pātam - patam - L. pedis (foot) - F. pie
uṭiya - uṭi - ma. uṭe - L. de.
kuruku - L. grus - F. grue, gru.  kuruku = kokku, nāri, ōtimam (aṉṉam).
koṭivaḻip pirivuk kōṭu kokkukkāli ottiruttalāl, koṭivaḻi kurukukkāl eṉap peyar peṟṟatu.
  4.   E. 	pedagogy, science of teaching, nuvaṟcinūl, 			kaṟpippu nūl.
E.  pedagogue, schoolmaster, teacher. Gk. pidagogos. Gk. pidos, boy, agogos f. ago, to lead.
pital - pidos, uki - aki - ago,. pital =  piyaṉ, akittal = celuttutal, naṭattal.
Gk. pidagogos-pidagogia = E. pedagogy.
5.   E.	allotrophy,  variation of physical properties without 		change of substance.
allatu - Gk. allos. tiruppu - Gk. trepo.
iṅṅaṉam palvēṟu kuṟiyīṭṭuk coṟkaḷ maṭṭumaṉṟi, technical term eṉṉum kalikkuṟiyīṭṭup potuppeyarum tamiḻc collāyiruppatu viyantu makiḻattakkatu.
ti - ticcu - taccu = pala marattuṇṭukaḷi iṇittuc ceyyum vēli. taccu - taccaṉ = joiner, carpenter. aivakik kolvēlikaḷuḷ mutalatu taccē. poṉṉuñ cempum irumpuṅ kaṇṭupiṭikkumuṉ, kalliṟkuppiṉ, marattiyē taṭṭumuṭṭukaṭku makkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiyiruttal vēṇṭum. koltoḻil eṉpatu koli viṉi. uyiruḷḷa poru ḷiyē kollutal kūṭum. maram uyiruḷḷatu. ātalāl taccar marakkollar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. maraṅkoḵ ṟaccarum eṉṟu cilappatikāraṅ (5 : 29) kūṟutal kāṇka. kol, kam(kammiyam) eṉpaṉapōl, taccu eṉpatu mutaṟkālattiṟ kaliyik (art) kuṟittatu.
taccaṉ - Gk tekton (joiner, carpenter), Gk. tekhne(art) - Gk. tekhnikos-F. technic, E  technic, technique, technical.
tuḷ - tura(tuḷi) - tuṟa - tuṟappu - tuṟappaṇam =  tuḷiyiṭu karuvi (drill). tuṟa - tiṟa. tuṟappu - tiṟappu, tuṟavu - tiṟavu, tuṟappu = tiṟavukōl. tuṟappukkuccu eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka.
Gk. trupo, to bore, trupa, hole - Gk. trupanon - L. trepanum - OF. trepan(er) - ME, E. trepan, borer, surgeon’s cylindrical saw. tuṟappaṇam - Gk. trupanon. iṅṅaṉam taccuvēlik karuvippeyarum ottiruttal kāṇka.
tīrtal = muṭital. tīrttal = muṭittal. tīrmāṉam = muṭivu, muṭipu, iciyil tāḷamuṭivu allatu mataṅkam (mirutaṅkam) pōṉṟa tōṟ karuvittaṭṭu muṭippu. māṉam oru toḻiṟpeyarīṟu o.nō: cēr - cērmāṉam. tīrmāṉam - tīrmāṉi. tīrmāṉittal = muṭivu ceytal, tāḷan tīrttal.
L.terminus, end, limit, boundary.
L. terminus-OF. terme - ME, E. term, limit of time, limited period, period during which instruction is given in schools and colleges, estate or interest in land to be enjoyed for fixed period, word used to express a definite  conception, language employed, mode of expression, conditions of agreement or reference.
E. terminate, to come or bring to an end.
L. determinare - F. determiner - E. determine, to bring or come to an end, limit in scope, define, fix beforehand, settle, decide. L. de - poruḷi niṟivikkum allatu mikutippaṭuttum oru muṉṉoṭṭu.
ittakiya coṟkaḷ ērāḷamāka vuḷḷaṉa. iṅṅaṉa mirukkavum, aṟiviyaṟ kuṟiyīṭukaḷi yellām tamiḻil moḻipeyarkkap  pōtiya collilliyeṉṟō, oru vakiyālum moḻipeyarkka muṭiyāteṉṟō, colvatu aṟiyāmiyiṉ pāṟpaṭṭatē. tammip pōṟ piṟariyuṅ karutit tamakkut teriyāta tellām piṟarukkun teriyā teṉṟu koḷvatu, palariṉ piṟavikkuṇamāyuḷḷatu.
iṉi, pala mēliyāriyak kuṟiyīṭukaḷum ciṟappuc coṟ kaḷum, poruḷaḷavil mika vuyarntaviyēṉum, collaḷavil mika eḷiyaviyāy iruppatum kavaṉikkattakkatākum. avaṟṟuṭ cila varumāṟu:

iṅṅaṉam pala vakikaḷik kiyāṇṭāl, ellāk kuṟiyīṭukaḷiyum moḻipeyarttuviṭalām. oru col allatu kuṟiyīṭu oru poruḷik kuṟikkuñ collā yiruntāṟ pōtum; vaṇṇikkuñ collāyirukka vēṇṭiya tilli; iruttaluṅ kūṭātu. oru pirāmaṇar kuḻu.  census eṉṉuñ collic cicupāla virutta stirīpuruṣa kulamata virutti caṅkiyā eṉṟu moḻipeyarttatu. kuṭimatippu eṉṉuñ col ēṟkeṉavē uḷḷatu. atuvē pōtum. Collector eṉpati māvaṭṭa āṭcittalivar eṉpatum vaṭṭa vaḻic collē. taṇṭalāḷar eṉpatē takkatām.
oru cila kuṟiyīṭukaḷi oliyotta vakiyilum moḻi peyarkkalām. e-ṭu: parliament - pārāḷumaṉṟu, bracket - piṟikkōṭu.
moḻikaḷ ceṭikoṭikaḷippōla virivaḷarika ḷalla; maraṅkaḷippōl mella vaḷarikaḷ. āṅkilam toṭakkan toṭṭu 15 nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka vaḷarntu varukiṉ ṟatu. camaṟkirutam eṉṉum ilakkiya moḻi kaṭanta mūvāyiram āṇṭāka vaḷarntu varukiṉṟatu. āriyar teṉṉāṭu vantapiṉ tamiḻ vaḷarcci taṭippaṭuttappaṭṭu viṭṭatu. atōṭu āyirakkaṇakkāṉa coṟkaḷ maṟintum vaḻakku vīḻttap paṭṭum pōyiṉa. āyiṉum aṟiviyaṟ kuṟiyīṭukaḷi moḻi peyarkkap pōtiya karuvic coṟkaḷ iṉṟum uḷḷaṉa. poṟumi maṭṭum vēṇṭum. iṟṟit tamiḻvaḷarcci āṅkilarāṭciyāl ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭē toṭaṅkiṟṟu. maṟimaliyaṭikaḷiṉ māṭcimiku toṇṭiṉāl atu māperu vēkam peṟṟatu. moḻipeyarppu ceyaṟki yāyirātu iyaṟkiyāyiruttal vēṇṭum.

          	cycle - mitivaṇṭi		irucakkara vaṇṭi
          	funnel - vittūṟṟi		virivāykkuḻal
centamiḻc coṟpiṟappiyal akara mutaliyiṉ 12ām maṭalam centamiḻc coṟpiṟappiyal neṟimuṟikaḷ (Principles of Tamil Etymology) atu putuc collākka muṟikaḷiyum moḻipeyarppu muṟikaḷiyum teḷḷatteḷivākak kāṭṭum. atip piṉpaṟṟi ellāc coṟkaḷiyum eḷitāy moḻi peyarkkalām. atuvari pataṟṟamum paraparappumiṉṟip poṟumiyāyiruppatu naṉṟu. pataṟāta karumam citaṟātu. pataṟic ceyyum karumam citaṟik keṭṭuppōkum. kuṟuṅkāla vaḷarikaḷ kuṟuvāḻikaḷākavum neṭuṅkāla vaḷarikaḷ nīṭuvāḻikaḷākavum irukkum.
collākkak kuḻu
tamiḻ tūymiyi uyirnāṭiyākak koṇṭiruppa taṉālum, mēṉmēlum pala ayaṟcoṟkaḷi iṭiviṭātu moḻipeyarkka vēṇṭiyiruppataṉālum, taṉippaṭṭa pulavarum māṇavar kuḻāṅkaḷum aṟiñar kuḻukkaḷum oru poruṭku vevvēṟu colli ākki vaḻaṅkuvataṉālum; avaṟṟuḷ eti āḷvateṉṟu teriyātu potu makkaḷ mayaṅkuvataṉālum, ēṟkeṉavē araciṉāl amarttappaṭṭa moḻipeyarppuk kuḻu  oru tuṟikkē ciṟappākavuriya tāyiruppataṉālum, tamiḻvaḷarccikku iṉṟiyamiyāta potuc coṟkaḷi yellām avvappōtu puṉintu vaḻakkāṟṟup paṭuttaṟkup palatuṟi  yaṟiñarik koṇṭa oru niliyāṉa collākkak kuḻuvi aracu ēṟpaṭuttuvatu naṉṟākum.
tamiḻiṉ talimiyi oppukkoḷḷal
vaṭamoḻi vañcaṉiyāl tamiḻiṉ ariyaṇiyik kavarntuḷḷatu. av vaṉkavarvu mūvāyiram āṇṭu toṭar ntu viṭṭataṉāṟ caṭṭamuṟi valimi peṟṟu viṭātu. nīṇṭa nāṭpaṭṭa nōyāṉālum nīkkiyē tīravēṇṭum. oru nāṭṭiluḷḷa vantēṟikaḷ nākarikarāṉa paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷip piṉpaṟṟiyē vāḻtal vēṇṭum; tām nilattēvareṉṟu, peyakkaṇṭum nañcuṇṭamiyum nayattakka nākarikam vēṇṭum tamiḻmakkaḷi ēmāṟṟit tāḻttiyatoṭu tamiḻiyuṅ keṭuppatu, uytiyillāc ceytikkoliyākum (When you are at Rome do as Rome does).
eṉṟikkum ellāriyum Vkh‰wKoahJ(Cheating  play never thrives). vantāri vāḻvittu iruntāri iṟappikkum tamiḻar poṟumikkum ōr elliyuṇṭu. av velli kaṭantuviṭṭatu. eṇṇeyum uṇmiyum iṟutiyil mēṟ paṭum  (Oil and truth will get uppermost at last.). ātalāl, tamiḻiṉ talimiyit tamiḻnāṭṭaracu maṭṭumaṉṟi naṭuvaṇaracum oppukkoḷḷal vēṇṭum. tamiḻ  mīṇṭum ariyaṇiyēṟi, tamiḻar tamiḻarallātār eṉṉum vēṟupāṭiṉṟit tamiḻ nāṭṭārellārum orumittu vāḻtal vēṇṭum.
tamiḻppulavar poṟuppu
tamiḻik kāppatu tamiḻnāṭṭaraciṉ taṉikkaṭami. atu ak kaṭami tavaṟiṉ, nakkīrarum, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷum, cōma cuntara pāratiyārum, ilakkuvaṉārumpōl tamiḻip pōṟṟik kāppatu iṟṟit talimit tamiḻp pērāciriyar talimēl viḻunta taliyāya kaṭamiyām.
mēliyāriyar tamiḻccoṟkaḷi āṇṭavāṟē nāmum āḷa vēṇṭumeṉṟum, collākkattil avar kiyāṇṭa neṟimuṟikaḷiyellām nāmum kiyāḷa vēṇṭu meṉṟum yāppuṟavilli. nam muṉṉōr marapip piṉ paṟṟiyē nām coṟkaḷi ākkik koḷḷutal vēṇṭum. kalik kuṟiyīṭukaḷit tamiḻil moḻi peyarkka muṭiyā teṉpār kūṟṟi maṟukkavē, itukāṟuṅ kūṟappaṭṭateṉa aṟika.
* * * * *
makkaḷ vāḻttu
tēva moḻiyeṉun teṭṭi nīkkiyē
ēva runtamiḻ ematu moḻiyeṉa
mēvi vāḻiyar mēli maṟantapiṉ
tāvil nallicit tamiḻam ōṅkavē.
uḷḷa naṟṟoḻil uñaṟṟit tammuṭip
paḷḷi yiruntuṇāp pakirntuṇ ṭiṉpamāyt
teḷḷu kaṟpuṭit tēvi yōṭaṟam
vaḷḷu varmati vakutta vāḻvarō.
mikka piṟappiṉi miṇṭit taṭuttapiṉ
takka aṟiñarit taliva rākkiyē
okka oṉṟiya vulaka āṭciyil 
makkaḷ vāḻiyar maṇṇil viṇṇitē.
